你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game（Terraria 1.4.5.6 复刻，种子等价工程）。任务：**165 族装饰位置漂移的剩余贡献 pass 清剿**——VV 批修了 Stalac 段（placeTight 初始列），但 s111 矩阵显示 165↔-1 双向 ~29k 格（VV 修后预期降至 ~15k）——需要找到其余贡献 pass。

## 禁区
tests/golden/*、tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs（YY 独占）、src/world/gen/vanilla/{DungeonPass,TemplePass,HellFortPass,CaveWallsPass,HalfBrickPass,MicroBiomesPass,MarbleGranitePass,ShimmerPass,LiquidSim,CaveHousePass,BuriedChestsPass,WebsPass,HiveSpiderPass}.ts（YY 或他人域）。GemPasses.ts/StructuresPass.ts/MossPass.ts/装饰实现可改。

## 背景
- tile 165 = cave decoration（icicle/stalactite 族）
- 贡献 pass：#64 GemCaves（Spread.Gem 的 1/2 暴露晶簇）、#65 Moss（PlaceTile184 长苔藓）、#92 ExposedGems（冰系+地下+琥珀三段）、#103 Stalac（VV 已修 placeTight 初始列）——165 也被多个 pass 消费
- VV 修了 #103 的 placeTight，但 #64/#65/#92 各自的放置路径可能也有位置漂移
- **QQ 备案：JS QuickCleanupPass (b) setTileSilent 不清 half/slope**（原版显式清——island 链哈希不可见但全链转录后显形）——这也是 165 位置漂移的候选

## 方法论
1. 跑 SW_MATRIX_VSRV=/tmp/vserver86 SW_MATRIX=small8 SW_MATRIX_FORCE=1 看当前 165↔-1 实际格数（VV 修后）
2. 对每个候选 pass 隔离重放（GemPasses/MossPass/StructuresPass 各自 rig），定位哪几个还在偏
3. 修复→复验

## 纪律
单次<8分钟；金标零写；探针杀净。

88	}

90	function loadRows(): Row[] {
91	  const lines = readFileSync(TSV, 'utf8').split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() && !l.startsWith('file\t'));
92	  const rows: Row[] = [];
93	  for (const line of lines) {
94	    const c = line.split('\t');
95	    if (c.length < 13) continue;
96	    rows.push({
97	      file: c[0], size: c[1] as Row['size'], seed: c[2], evil: c[3] === '1' ? 1 : 0,
98	      ground: Number(c[4]), rock: Number(c[5]), worldId: Number(c[6]), moon: Number(c[7]),
99	      dungeonX: Number(c[8]), dungeonY: Number(c[9]), spawnX: Number(c[10]), worldIdDraw: Number(c[12]),
100	    });
101	  }
102	  return rows;
103	}
104	
105	function selectRows(rows: Row[]): Row[] {
106	  if (MODE.startsWith('list:')) {
107	    const want = new Set(MODE.slice(5).split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean));
108	    return rows.filter((r) => want.has(r.file));
109	  }
110	  if (MODE === 'all') return rows;
111	  if (MODE === 'smoke') {
112	    const s = rows.filter((r) => r.size === 's').slice(0, 8);
113	    const m = rows.filter((r) => r.size === 'm').slice(0, 2);
114	    const l = rows.filter((r) => r.size === 'l').slice(0, 2);
115	    return [...s, ...m, ...l];
116	  }
117	  // small / small8 / 1 → 小世界前 8（baseline 档）
118	  return rows.filter((r) => r.size === 's').slice(0, 8);
119	}
120	
121	// ---- worldId 锚（RNG 重放）：genRand 原始流第 t 个样本按 Next(int.MaxValue) 重放 ----
122	// （与 tools/_matrixdump.test.ts 同法；生成期 ⑫ WorldId 抽样在 JS 侧掷而未存——重放即 JS 侧真值）
123	function replayWorldId(seedText: string, t: number): { pos: number; value: number } {
124	  const r = new UnifiedRandom(translateSeed(seedText));
125	  let last = 0;
126	  for (let i = 1; i <= t; i++) last = Math.trunc(r.next() * SAMPLE_SCALE * INT_MAX);
127	  return { pos: t, value: last };
128	}
129	
130	// ---- 稀疏对象计数：连通棵数 + 树干 run（长 ≥4 的竖直连续段）----
131	interface SparseCount { cc: number; cells: number; trunkRuns: number; trunkCols: number }
132	function countSparse(
133	  w: number, h: number, visited: Uint8Array, isTreeFamily: boolean,
134	  member: (i: number) => boolean,
135	): SparseCount {
136	  visited.fill(0);
137	  let cc = 0, cells = 0, trunkRuns = 0;
138	  const trunkColHit = new Uint8Array(w);
139	  let trunkCols = 0;
140	  const stack: number[] = [];
141	  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
142	    if (visited[i] || !member(i)) continue;
143	    cc++;
144	    stack.length = 0; stack.push(i);
145	    visited[i] = 1;
146	    while (stack.length) {
147	      const j = stack.pop()!;
148	      cells++;
149	      const x = j % w, y = (j / w) | 0;
150	      // 4 邻
151	      if (x > 0 && !visited[j - 1] && member(j - 1)) { visited[j - 1] = 1; stack.push(j - 1); }
152	      if (x < w - 1 && !visited[j + 1] && member(j + 1)) { visited[j + 1] = 1; stack.push(j + 1); }
153	      if (y > 0 && !visited[j - w] && member(j - w)) { visited[j - w] = 1; stack.push(j - w); }
154	      if (y < h - 1 && !visited[j + w] && member(j + w)) { visited[j + w] = 1; stack.push(j + w); }
155	    }
156	  }
157	  if (isTreeFamily) {
158	    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
159	      let y = 0;
160	      while (y < h) {
161	        const i = y * w + x;
162	        if (member(i) && (y === 0 || !member(i - w))) {
163	          let len = 0;
164	          while (y + len < h && member((y + len) * w + x)) len++;
165	          if (len >= 4) { trunkRuns++; if (!trunkColHit[x]) { trunkColHit[x] = 1; trunkCols++; } }
166	          y += len;
167	        } else y++;
168	      }
169	    }
170	  }
171	  return { cc, cells, trunkRuns, trunkCols };
172	}
173	
174	interface ZoneStat {
175	  cells: number; typ: number; act: number; wal: number; liq: number;
176	  typTop: Array<[string, number]>; walTop: Array<[string, number]>; liqTop: Array<[string, number]>;
177	}
178	function topN(m: Map<string, number>, n: number): Array<[string, number]> {
179	  return [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, n);
180	}
181	
182	interface WorldReport {
183	  file: string; size: string; seed: string; evil: number;
184	  genSeconds: number; parseSeconds: number; at: string;
185	  error?: string;
186	  header?: Record<string, { v: number | boolean | number[]; j: number | boolean | number[]; d: number | string }>;
187	  sparse?: Record<string, { v: number | number[]; j: number | number[] }>;
188	  zones?: Record<string, ZoneStat & { typPct: number; walPct: number; liqPct: number }>;
189	  overall?: { cells: number; typ: number; act: number; wal: number; liq: number; typPct: number; walPct: number; liqPct: number; unmappedJsActiveCells: number };
190	}
191	
192	function anchor<T extends number | boolean | number[]>(v: T, j: T): { v: T; j: T; d: number | string } {
193	  const eq = Array.isArray(v) && Array.isArray(j)
194	    ? v.length === j.length && v.every((x, i) => x === j[i])
195	    : v === j;
196	  return { v, j, d: eq ? 0 : '≠' };
197	}
198	
199	async function runWorld(row: Row): Promise<WorldReport> {
200	  const t0 = Date.now();
201	  const base: WorldReport = { file: row.file, size: row.size, seed: row.seed, evil: row.evil, genSeconds: 0, parseSeconds: 0, at: new Date().toISOString() };
202	  const tParse = Date.now();
203	  const wld: WldWorld = parseWld(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`${VSRV}/${row.file}`)));
204	  const parseSeconds = (Date.now() - tParse) / 1000;
205	  const tGen = Date.now();
206	  const dims = SIZE_DIMS[SIZE_ID[row.size]];
207	  const world: World = await generateWorld({
208	    size: SIZE_ID[row.size], width: dims.width, height: dims.height,
209	    seedText: row.seed, evil: row.evil,
210	  });
211	  const genSeconds = (Date.now() - tGen) / 1000;
212	  const st = world.store;
213	  const w = wld.tilesWide, h = wld.tilesHigh, n = w * h;
214	  if (st.w !== w || st.h !== h) { base.error = `尺寸不符 js=${st.w}x${st.h} wld=${w}x${h}`; return base; }
215	  base.parseSeconds = parseSeconds;
216	  base.genSeconds = genSeconds;
217	
218	  // ① 头部锚（快验）——worldId 走 RNG 重放（js 侧真值 = 重放值）
219	  const wid = replayWorldId(row.seed, row.worldIdDraw || 18);
220	  base.header = {
221	    ground: anchor(wld.groundLevel, world.groundLevel),
222	    rock: anchor(wld.rockLevel, world.rockLevel),
223	    moon: anchor(wld.moonType, world.moonType),
224	    dungeonX: anchor(wld.dungeonX, world.dungeonX),
225	    dungeonY: anchor(wld.dungeonY, world.dungeonY),
226	    spawnX: anchor(wld.spawnX, world.spawnX),
227	    spawnY: anchor(wld.spawnY, world.spawnY),
228	    crimson: anchor(wld.isCrimson, world.crimson),
229	    treeX: anchor(wld.treeX, world.treeX ?? []),
230	    treeStyle: anchor(wld.treeStyle, world.treeStyle ?? []),
231	    worldId: anchor(wld.worldId, wid.value),
232	  };
233	
234	  // ② 稀疏对象逐棵计数（两侧同口径：投影到原版 id 后 CC / trunk-run）
235	  const wt = wld.tiles, jt = st;
236	  const vanActive = (i: number) => wt.active[i] === 1;
237	  const jsActive = (i: number) => jt.type[i] !== 0;
238	  const visited = new Uint8Array(n);
239	  // 两侧同函数体、同口径（visited 复用，每次 countSparse 开头 fill(0) 重置）
240	  const mk = (lut: Uint8Array, tree: boolean) => ({
241	    v: countSparse(w, h, visited, tree, (i) => vanActive(i) && lut[wt.type[i]] === 1),
242	    j: countSparse(w, h, visited, tree, (i) => {
243	      if (!jsActive(i)) return false;
244	      const v = SHEET[jt.type[i]];
245	      return v >= 0 && lut[v] === 1;
246	    }),
247	  });
248	  base.sparse = {
249	    trees: mk(LUT_TREE, true),
250	    palms: mk(LUT_PALM, false),
251	    chests: { v: wld.chests.length, j: world.chests.length },
252	    statues: mk(LUT_STATUE, false),
253	    pots: mk(LUT_POT, false),
254	  };
255	
256	  // ③ 分区四通道差（单遍全图扫描）
257	  const gl = Math.floor(wld.groundLevel), rl = Math.floor(wld.rockLevel), hellTop = h - 200;
258	  const caveEnd = rl + ((hellTop - rl) >> 1);
259	  const bandOf = new Uint8Array(h);
260	  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) bandOf[y] = y < gl - 60 ? 0 : y < rl ? 1 : y < caveEnd ? 2 : y < hellTop ? 3 : 4;
261	  const zones = BAND_NAMES.map(() => ({
262	    cells: 0, typ: 0, act: 0, wal: 0, liq: 0,
263	    typM: new Map<string, number>(), walM: new Map<string, number>(), liqM: new Map<string, number>(),
264	  }));
265	  const overall = { cells: n, typ: 0, act: 0, wal: 0, liq: 0, typPct: 0, walPct: 0, liqPct: 0, unmappedJsActiveCells: 0 };
266	  const bump = (m: Map<string, number>, k: string) => { m.set(k, (m.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); };
267	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
268	    const z = zones[bandOf[(i / w) | 0]];
269	    z.cells++;
270	    const va = wt.active[i] === 1;
271	    const jType = jt.type[i];
272	    const ja = jType !== 0;
273	    const vt = va ? wt.type[i] : -1;
274	    let jtV = -1;
275	    if (ja) {
276	      const s = SHEET[jType];
277	      if (s >= 0) jtV = s; else { jtV = -2; overall.unmappedJsActiveCells++; }
278	    }
279	    if (va !== ja) { z.act++; overall.act++; }
280	    if (va !== ja || (va && vt !== jtV)) {
281	      z.typ++; overall.typ++;
282	      bump(z.typM, `${vt}>${jtV === -2 ? 'UNMAPPED' : jtV}`);
283	    }
284	    const vw = wt.wall[i], jw = jt.wall[i];
285	    if (vw !== jw) { z.wal++; overall.wal++; bump(z.walM, `${vw}>${jw}`); }
286	    const vamt = wt.liquid[i], jamt = jt.liquid[i];
287	    const vlt = vamt > 0 ? wt.liquidType[i] : 0;
288	    const jlt = jamt > 0 ? jt.liquidType[i] : 0;
289	    if (vamt !== jamt || vlt !== jlt) {
290	      z.liq++; overall.liq++;
291	      bump(z.liqM, `t${vlt}:${vamt}>t${jlt}:${jamt}`);
292	    }
293	  }
294	  overall.typPct = (overall.typ / n) * 100;
295	  overall.walPct = (overall.wal / n) * 100;
296	  overall.liqPct = (overall.liq / n) * 100;
297	  base.zones = {};
298	  BAND_NAMES.forEach((name, bi) => {
299	    const z = zones[bi];
300	    base.zones![name] = {
301	      cells: z.cells, typ: z.typ, act: z.act, wal: z.wal, liq: z.liq,
302	      typTop: topN(z.typM, 5), walTop: topN(z.walM, 5), liqTop: topN(z.liqM, 5),
303	      typPct: (z.typ / z.cells) * 100, walPct: (z.wal / z.cells) * 100, liqPct: (z.liq / z.cells) * 100,
304	    };
305	  });
306	  base.overall = overall;
307	  return base;
308	}
309	
310	// ---- 报告累积 + 汇总打印 ----
311	interface Report { meta: Record<string, unknown>; worlds: Record<string, WorldReport> }
312	function loadReport(): Report {
313	  if (FRESH || !existsSync(OUT)) return { meta: {}, worlds: {} };
314	  try {
315	    const r = JSON.parse(readFileSync(OUT, 'utf8'));
316	    return { meta: r.meta ?? {}, worlds: r.worlds ?? {} };
317	  } catch { return { meta: {}, worlds: {} }; }
318	}
319	
320	function fmtPct(x: number): string { return x >= 0.1 ? x.toFixed(2) : x.toExponential(1); }
321	function dv(d: number | string): string { return d === 0 ? '0' : String(d); }
322	
323	function printSummary(rep: Report) {
324	  const entries = Object.values(rep.worlds);
325	  if (!entries.length) { console.log('（报告为空）'); return; }
326	  entries.sort((a, b) => 'sml'.indexOf(a.size) - 'sml'.indexOf(b.size) || a.file.localeCompare(b.file));
327	  console.log(`\n===== 收敛矩阵汇总（${entries.length} 世界，${OUT}）=====`);
328	  console.log('file            gen(s)  hdrΔ | trunk v/j      treeCC v/j     chest v/j  statue v/j  pot v/j  | typ%      wal%      liq%');
329	  let typSum = 0, walSum = 0, liqSum = 0, ok = 0;
330	  for (const e of entries) {
331	    if (e.error) { console.log(`${e.file.padEnd(16)} ERROR: ${e.error}`); continue; }
332	    const hdrBad = Object.values(e.header!).filter((a) => a.d !== 0).length;
333	    const sp = e.sparse!;
334	    const t = sp.trees, c = sp.chests, stt = sp.statues, p = sp.pots;
335	    const o = e.overall!;
336	    console.log(
337	      `${e.file.padEnd(16)} ${e.genSeconds.toFixed(0).padStart(5)}  ${String(hdrBad).padStart(3)} | `
338	      + `${String(t.v.trunkRuns).padStart(5)}/${String(t.j.trunkRuns).padEnd(5)} `
339	      + `${String(t.v.cc).padStart(5)}/${String(t.j.cc).padEnd(5)} `
340	      + `${String(c.v).padStart(4)}/${String(c.j).padEnd(4)} `
341	      + `${String(stt.v.cc).padStart(4)}/${String(stt.j.cc).padEnd(4)} `
342	      + `${String(p.v.cc).padStart(5)}/${String(p.j.cc).padEnd(5)} | `
343	      + `${fmtPct(o.typPct).padStart(9)} ${fmtPct(o.walPct).padStart(9)} ${fmtPct(o.liqPct).padStart(9)}`,
344	    );
345	    typSum += o.typPct; walSum += o.walPct; liqSum += o.liqPct; ok++;
346	  }
347	  if (ok) {
348	    console.log(`${'-'.repeat(120)}`);
349	    console.log(`均值（${ok} 世界）：typ%=${(typSum / ok).toFixed(3)} wal%=${(walSum / ok).toFixed(3)} liq%=${(liqSum / ok).toFixed(3)}`);
350	  }
351	  // 分区 typ 细目矩阵
352	  console.log('\nfile            | sky.t%    surface.t%  cave.t%    deep.t%    hell.t%   | sky.w%  surf.w% cave.w% deep.w% hell.w%');
353	  for (const e of entries) {
354	    if (e.error || !e.zones) continue;
355	    const z = BAND_NAMES.map((b) => e.zones![b]);
356	    console.log(
357	      `${e.file.padEnd(16)} | `
358	      + z.map((s) => fmtPct(s.typPct).padStart(10)).join(' ') + ' | '
359	      + z.map((s) => fmtPct(s.walPct).padStart(7)).join(' '),
360	    );
361	  }
362	  // 每世界最热差异对（typ 最差带的 top3）
363	  console.log('\n—— 每世界 typ 差异最重的带 top3 差异对（v原版>j本仓）——');
364	  for (const e of entries) {
365	    if (e.error || !e.zones) continue;
366	    const worst = BAND_NAMES.map((b) => ({ b, s: e.zones![b] })).sort((a, b2) => b2.s.typ - a.s.typ)[0];
367	    const top = worst.s.typTop.slice(0, 3).map(([k, c2]) => `${k}×${c2}`).join(', ');
368	    console.log(`${e.file.padEnd(16)} ${worst.b}(typ=${worst.s.typ}/${worst.s.cells}) ${top || '-'}`);
369	  }
370	}
371	
372	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('convergence matrix run', async () => {
373	  if (MODE === 'report') { printSummary(loadReport()); return; }
374	  const rows = loadRows();
375	  if (!rows.length) throw new Error(`${TSV} 无数据行`);
376	  const sel = selectRows(rows);
377	  const rep = loadReport();
378	  const t0 = Date.now();
379	  let ran = 0;
380	  console.log(`SW_MATRIX=${MODE} 选中 ${sel.length} 世界（MAX=${MAX} 预算=${BUDGET_MS / 1000}s 已有=${Object.keys(rep.worlds).length}）`);
381	  for (const row of sel) {
382	    if (ran >= MAX) { console.log(`已达本次 MAX=${MAX}，余量下次续跑`); break; }
383	    if (!FORCE && rep.worlds[row.file]?.overall) { console.log(`跳过（报告已有）：${row.file}`); continue; }
384	    const elapsed = Date.now() - t0;
385	    const expect = EXPECT_S[row.size] ?? 300;
386	    if (ran > 0 && elapsed + expect * 1000 > BUDGET_MS) {
387	      console.log(`预算守门：已用 ${elapsed / 1000 | 0}s + 预估 ${expect}s 超预算，止于 ${ran} 世界`);
388	      break;
389	    }
390	    console.log(`▶ ${row.file}（seed=${row.seed} evil=${row.evil} 预估 ${expect}s）…`);
391	    try {
392	      const entry = await runWorld(row);
393	      rep.worlds[row.file] = entry;
394	      if (entry.error) console.log(`  ✗ ${entry.error}`);
395	      else {
396	        console.log(
397	          `  ✓ gen=${entry.genSeconds!.toFixed(0)}s typ%=${entry.overall!.typPct.toFixed(3)} wal%=${entry.overall!.walPct.toFixed(3)} liq%=${entry.overall!.liqPct.toFixed(3)}`
398	          + ` trunk ${entry.sparse!.trees.v.trunkRuns}/${entry.sparse!.trees.j.trunkRuns}`
399	          + ` chest ${entry.sparse!.chests.v}/${entry.sparse!.chests.j}`
400	          + ` statue ${entry.sparse!.statues.v.cc}/${entry.sparse!.statues.j.cc}`
401	          + ` pot ${entry.sparse!.pots.v.cc}/${entry.sparse!.pots.j.cc}`
402	          + ` unmapped=${entry.overall!.unmappedJsActiveCells}`,
403	        );
404	      }
405	    } catch (err) {
406	      rep.worlds[row.file] = {
407	        ...{ file: row.file, size: row.size, seed: row.seed, evil: row.evil, genSeconds: 0, parseSeconds: 0, at: new Date().toISOString() },
408	        error: (err as Error).message,
409	      };
410	      console.log(`  ✗ 异常：${(err as Error).message}`);
411	    }
412	    ran++;
413	    // 滚动修正耗时预估（同尺寸）
414	    const done = rep.worlds[row.file];
415	    if (done && !done.error) EXPECT_S[row.size] = Math.round(done.genSeconds * 1.15);
416	    writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(rep, null, 1));
417	  }
418	  rep.meta = { ...rep.meta, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), mode: MODE, count: Object.keys(rep.worlds).length };
419	  writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(rep, null, 1));
420	  printSummary(rep);
421	  console.log(`\n报告已写 ${OUT}（累计 ${Object.keys(rep.worlds).length} 世界）`);
422	}, 3_600_000);
423

205	function checkMultiDirAnchor(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number): number {
206	  const belowOk = validAnchor(st, i, j + 1, 1);
207	  const leftOk = validAnchor(st, i - 1, j, 2);
208	  const rightOk = validAnchor(st, i + 1, j, 3);
209	  const aboveOk = validAnchor(st, i, j - 1, 0);
210	  const solidNonTop = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {

214	    return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform;
215	  };
216	  if (belowOk && solidNonTop(i, j + 1)) return 0;
217	  if (leftOk && solidNonTop(i - 1, j)) return 2;
218	  if (rightOk && solidNonTop(i + 1, j)) return 3;
219	  if (aboveOk && solidNonTop(i, j - 1)) return 1;

259	}

261	export function runGemCavesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
262	  // 裸 double 比较（cs:17534：`for i < w*0.003`）——不取整，12.6 也进 13 次
263	  const num = st.w * 0.003;
264	  // nextCount（WorldGen.cs:9506-9581）：洪泛连通空气腔；wall!=0 即封顶；lavaOk=false
265	  // 时岩浆格 lavaCount++ 且立即封顶（含空气格岩浆）；ice 只认 147/161
266	  // ★零风险优化(2026-08-13):原 Set<number>+元组数组 → 平坦 Int32Array 栈 +
267	  // Uint8Array seen(visited 列表局部清除)。push 四邻顺序与 pop 语义逐点保持
268	  // (DFS 下邻优先),掷骰/判定序列不变。
269	  const seenArr = new Uint8Array(st.w * st.h);
270	  const visited: number[] = [];
271	  const stackX = new Int32Array(4096);
272	  const stackY = new Int32Array(4096);
273	  const countTiles = (sx: number, sy: number): { air: number; lava: number; ice: number; rock: number } => {
274	    let air = 0, lava = 0, ice = 0, rock = 0;
275	    let sp = 0;
276	    stackX[sp] = sx; stackY[sp] = sy; sp++;
277	    while (sp > 0) {
278	      if (air >= 300) break;                                  // numTileCount >= maxTileCount(300)
279	      sp--;
280	      const x = stackX[sp], y = stackY[sp];
281	      if (x <= 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= st.h - 1) {
282	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
283	        visited.length = 0;
284	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
285	      }
286	      const ii = st.idx(x, y);
287	      if (seenArr[ii]) continue;
288	      seenArr[ii] = 1;
289	      visited.push(ii);
290	      if (st.wall[ii] === 244) {
291	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
292	        visited.length = 0;
293	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
294	      }
295	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER) {
296	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
297	        visited.length = 0;
298	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
299	      }
300	      if (st.wall[ii] !== 0) {
301	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
302	        visited.length = 0;
303	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };                  // jungle=false
304	      }
305	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) {
306	        lava++;
307	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
308	        visited.length = 0;
309	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
310	      }
311	      if (st.flags[ii]) { // 实心：只计数不扩散
312	        if (ICE_COUNT.has(st.type[ii])) ice++;
313	        else if (st.type[ii] === STONE) rock++;
314	      }
315	      if (!solidTileAt(st, x, y)) {           // cs:9584 !SolidTile(x,y) → 计数+扩散
316	        air++;
317	        // 四邻入栈(与原 push 顺序一致,pop 取尾 = 下邻先访)。
318	        // 栈深上界 = 4×300(air≤300,超即 break,仅 air 格入栈)+1 < 4096,无需增长
319	        stackX[sp] = x - 1; stackY[sp] = y; sp++;
320	        stackX[sp] = x + 1; stackY[sp] = y; sp++;
321	        stackX[sp] = x; stackY[sp] = y - 1; sp++;
322	        stackX[sp] = x; stackY[sp] = y + 1; sp++;
323	      }
324	    }
325	    for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
326	    visited.length = 0;
327	    return { air, lava, ice, rock };
328	  };
329	  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
330	    // 选点（:17547-17565）：x∈[200,w-200)、y∈[rockLevel+30, h-230)、
331	    // countTiles∈[50,300) 且 lavaCount==0 && iceCount==0 && rockCount>0（1000 次上限）
332	    let sx = -1, sy = -1;
333	    for (let tries = 0; tries < 1000; tries++) {
334	      const x = rng.nextIntRange(200, st.w - 200);
335	      const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel + 30, st.h - 230);
336	      const c = countTiles(x, y);
337	      if (c.air >= 50 && c.air < 300 && c.lava === 0 && c.ice === 0 && c.rock > 0) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
338	    }
339	    if (sx < 0) continue;
340	    // 选池（gemCave :9657-9673）：强制 1 种 + 各 1/6 追加（1~7 种）
341	    const gemOn = new Set<number>([rng.nextInt(6)]);
342	    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) if (rng.nextInt(6) === 0) gemOn.add(k);
343	    const randGem = () => { let n = rng.nextInt(6); while (!gemOn.has(n)) n = rng.nextInt(6); return n; };
344	    // randGemTile（:9685-9700）：19/20 石头，1/20 池内宝石
345	    const randGemTileId = () => (rng.nextInt(20) !== 0 ? STONE : GEM_TILES[randGem()]);
346	    // Spread.Gem（:3565-3651）1:1 BFS：
347	    //  实心格（或有墙空气格）→ 不扩散；仅 active 且 Gemmable 时自身+四邻转 randGemTile
348	    //  （空气+有墙格：if(tile.active()) 内零操作零掷，cs:3599-3619）；
349	    //  无墙空气格 → 墙 48+池选、1/2 暴露晶簇、四邻入队（神庙/房屋墙阻隔，天然不侵入）
350	    const seen = new Set<number>();
351	    let frontier: Array<[number, number]> = [[sx, sy]];
352	    while (frontier.length) {
353	      const next: Array<[number, number]> = [];
354	      for (const [x, y] of frontier) {
355	        if (x <= 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= st.h - 1) continue;
356	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
357	        if (seen.has(ii)) continue;
358	        seen.add(ii);
359	        if (solidTileAt(st, x, y) || st.wall[ii] !== 0) { // cs:3597 SolidTile||wall!=0
360	          if (!st.flags[ii]) continue;                    // 空气+有墙：零操作零掷
361	          if (GEMMABLE.has(st.type[ii])) st.type[ii] = randGemTileId();
362	          for (const [dx, dy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]] as const) {
363	            const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
364	            if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) continue;
365	            const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
366	            if (st.flags[ni] && GEMMABLE.has(st.type[ni])) st.type[ni] = randGemTileId();
367	          }
368	        } else {
369	          st.wall[ii] = GEM_WALL_BASE + randGem();
370	          // cs:3604：放置前另有 !tile.active() 门（&& 左求值，active 时不掷骰）——
371	          // 分支谓词改 SolidTile 后，半砖/坡/平台等 active 非实心格也会落到本分支，
372	          // 此门不可省（否则会覆盖活性格）
373	          if (!st.flags[ii] && rng.nextInt(2) === 0) placeExposed(st, rng, x, y, randGem());
374	          for (const [dx, dy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]] as const) {
375	            const ni = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
376	            if (!seen.has(ni)) next.push([x + dx, y + dy]);
377	          }
378	        }
379	      }
380	      frontier = next;
381	    }
382	  }
383	}
384	
385	// ============ ExposedGems 暴露晶簇（:20842-20913） ============
386	export function runExposedGemsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
387	  // 冰系（:20842-20872）：y∈[(ws+rl)/2, lavaLine)、x=逐行雪界 snowMinX/MaxX[y]；
388	  // 目标格自身为冰块（147/161/162/224）→ 先掷 4 个 Next(1,4) 窗口再掷 Next(12) style
389	  // （cs:20855-20859 掷序），周边不对称窗口的空气格放晶簇
390	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w * 0.25; i++) {
391	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), gs.lavaLine);
392	    if (y < 0 || y >= st.h) continue;
393	    const minX = gs.snowMinX?.[y] ?? gs.snowOriginLeft;
394	    const maxX = gs.snowMaxX?.[y] ?? gs.snowOriginRight;
395	    // cs:20851：x 掷骰**无条件**（snowMinX/MaxX 只填到 lavaLine-140，未填带读默认 0
396	    // 仍掷，Next(0,0)=0）——旧 `maxX<=minX continue` 吞掉必掷一骰，RNG 流分叉
397	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(minX, maxX);
398	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
399	    if (!st.flags[ii] || !ICE_FLOOR.has(st.type[ii])) continue;
400	    const wl = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
401	    const wr = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
402	    const wt = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
403	    const wb = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
404	    const style = randGemStyle(rng);
405	    for (let jx = x - wl; jx < x + wr; jx++) {
406	      for (let jy = y - wt; jy < y + wb; jy++) {
407	        if (jx < 40 || jx >= st.w - 40 || jy < 40 || jy >= st.h - 40) continue;
408	        const ji = st.idx(jx, jy);
409	        if (!st.flags[ji]) placeExposed(st, rng, jx, jy, style);
410	      }
411	    }
412	  }
413	  // 地下（:20874-20890）：w 次，x∈[20,w-20)、y∈[rockLevel, h-300)，空气+无岩浆+
414	  // 非地牢墙且墙≠27 → 单格（style=Next(12) 权重）
415	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w; i++) {
416	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(20, st.w - 20);
417	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel, st.h - 300);
418	    if (y < 0) continue;
419	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
420	    if (st.flags[ii]) continue;
421	    if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) continue; // anyLava（+1 编码）
422	    const wl = st.wall[ii];
423	    if (wl === 27 || WALL_DUNGEON.has(wl)) continue;
424	    placeExposed(st, rng, x, y, randGemStyle(rng));
425	  }
426	  // 琥珀（:20891-20913）：w 次，y∈[ws, h-300)，目标须空气+无岩浆+墙 187/216 →
427	  // 不对称窗口（各 1-3）的空气格放 style=6 晶簇。
428	  // 目标的 !active+!anyLava 门（cs:20901）曾缺失——实心沙岩也带 187/216 墙，
429	  // 命中率高一个数量级 → 琥珀簇 13× 金标（2026-08-12 用户标注）。
430	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w; i++) {
431	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(20, st.w - 20);
432	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 300);
433	    if (y < 0) continue;
434	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
435	    if (st.flags[ii]) continue;
436	    if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) continue; // anyLava（+1 编码，勿写 0）
437	    const wl = st.wall[ii];
438	    if (wl !== 187 && wl !== 216) continue;
439	    const n14 = () => 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
440	    const al = n14(), ar = n14(), at = n14(), ab = n14();
441	    for (let jx = x - al; jx < x + ar; jx++) {
442	      for (let jy = y - at; jy < y + ab; jy++) {
443	        if (!st.inBounds(jx, jy)) continue;
444	        const ji = st.idx(jx, jy);
445	        if (!st.flags[ji]) placeExposed(st, rng, jx, jy, 6);
446	      }
447	    }
448	  }
449	}
450	
451	// ============ SpeleothemsAndGemTrees 钟乳石+宝石树（:22196-22282） ============
452	export function runGemTreesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
453	  const g0 = globalThis as { __swSpCap?: (gs: GenState, st: TileStore) => void };
454	  try { g0.__swSpCap?.(gs, st); } catch { /* probe */ }
455	  const f = gs.seedFlags ?? {};
456	  const alwaysTry = !!(f.tenthAnniversary || f.drunkWorld);
457	  const oceanDepths = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
458	    if (y > (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40) return false;
459	    return x < gs.beachDistance || x > st.w - gs.beachDistance;
460	  };
461	  const sheetActive = (x: number, y: number): number => {
462	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
463	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
464	    return st.flags[i] ? SHEET[st.type[i]] : -1;
465	  };
466	  const rockFamily = (s: number): boolean =>
467	    STONE_FAMILY.has(s) || TILE_MOSS.has(s);
468	  for (let x = 20; x < st.w - 20; x++) {
469	    for (let y = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface); y < st.h - 20; y++) {
470	      // 宝石树段（:22208-22237）：掷骰在液体判定之前（短路与求值序 1:1）
471	      const treeRoll = alwaysTry ? true : rng.nextInt(5) === 0;
472	      if (treeRoll && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] === 0) {
473	        const treeType = idsByVanillaSheet(583 + rng.nextInt(7)).values().next().value ?? 0;
474	        if (treeType) growGemTree(st, rng, x, y, treeType);
475	      }
476	      // 地下钟乳石（:22239-22262）：非海洋、空格、1/5 → 上下邻石族清坡 + PlaceTight
477	      if (!oceanDepths(x, y) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
478	        const above = sheetActive(x, y - 1), below = sheetActive(x, y + 1);
479	        if (rockFamily(above) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) {
480	          st.slope[st.idx(x, y - 1)] = 0;
481	        }
482	        if (rockFamily(below) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)]) {
483	          st.slope[st.idx(x, y + 1)] = 0;
484	        }
485	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, y);
486	      }
487	    }
488	    // 地表钟乳石（:22264-22278）：k∈[5, worldSurface)；冰上 147/161、沙 25/203 上/下
489	    for (let k = 5; k < gs.worldSurface; k++) {
490	      const up = sheetActive(x, k - 1), down = sheetActive(x, k + 1);
491	      const clearAbove = () => {
492	        if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, k)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, k + 1)]) st.slope[st.idx(x, k - 1)] = 0;
493	      };
494	      if ((up === 147 || up === 161) && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
495	        clearAbove();
496	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, k);
497	      }
498	      if ((up === 25 || up === 203) && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
499	        clearAbove();
500	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, k);
501	      }
502	      if ((down === 25 || down === 203) && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
503	        if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, k)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, k - 1)]) st.slope[st.idx(x, k + 1)] = 0;
504	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, k);
505	      }
506	    }
507	  }
508	}
509	
510

20	const NEON_TILES = [539, 536, 534, 625].map((s) => [...idsByVanillaSheet(s)][0]);
21	/** 地狱氦苔藓 381（cs:17746-17770：25 半径框内岩浆格 >20 才换） */
22	const LAVA_MOSS = [...idsByVanillaSheet(381)][0];
23	/** 苔藓墙 = 54+mossType（WallID.CaveUnsafe 族，setMoss cs:9841） */
24	const MOSS_WALL_BASE = 54;
25	/** 霓虹苔藓洞禁区（cs:17598-17616 非 remix 分支）：砂岩/丛林草/蘑菇草/冰雪/
26	 *  硬化沙/地牢族——苔藓洞不与既有群系叠加 */
27	const NEON_FORBIDDEN = idsByVanillaSheet(70, 60, 367, 368, 161, 147, 396, 397);
28	/** Main.tileDungeon（Main.cs:7941-7946：41 青砖/43 蓝砖/44 绿砖/677-679 变体） */
29	const DUNGEON_TILES = idsByVanillaSheet(41, 43, 44, 677, 678, 679);
30	/** Main.tileMoss（Main.cs:6956-6996，按 sheet id）——尾段 SpreadGrass 的苔藓块判定 */
31	const MOSS_SHEETS = new Set([182, 180, 179, 381, 183, 181, 534, 536, 539, 625, 627]);
32	const SHIMMER_SAFETY = 150;            // shimmerSafetyDistance（WorldGen.cs:4104）
33	
34	/** WorldGen.SolidTile（cs:70155-70172）1:1：active && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop &&
35	 *  !halfBrick && slope==0（生成期无 inActive）。写法同源 CaveWallsPass.solidAt。
36	 *  ★M1/M2：旧版只查 flags+def.solid，丢 half/slope/solidTop——Spread.Moss(:3516) 与
37	 *  countTiles(:9584) 的"实心"判定与原版 SolidTile 分叉（half/slope 是逐格状态，
38	 *  不可按 id 预计算，必须逐格全谓词判定） */
39	const isSolidTile = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => {
40	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;   // Main.tile 索引器 null → true（SolidTile catch 语义）
41	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
42	  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
43	  const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
44	  if (!d) return false;
45	  return !!d.solid && !d.platform && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0;
46	};
47	
48	/** setMoss（cs:9838-9844）：按 x 三分世界选 mossType 槽 → 全局 mossWall/mossTile。
49	 *  本仓用返回值传递（GenVars 全局位） */
50	function setMoss(st: TileStore, x: number, mossType: number[]): { wall: number; tile: number } {
51	  const slot = x < st.w * 0.334 ? 0 : x < st.w * 0.667 ? 1 : 2;
52	  return { wall: MOSS_WALL_BASE + mossType[slot], tile: MOSS_TILES[mossType[slot]] };
53	}
54	
55	/** Spread.Moss（cs:3497-3549）1:1 BFS：起点洞腔涂苔藓墙。
56	 *  实心格或已有墙 → 不扩散；active 且无墙 → 补墙；石头(1) → 换苔藓块；
57	 *  无墙空气 → 涂墙并向四邻扩散（有墙/实心天然阻断） */
58	function spreadMoss(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, wall: number, tile: number): void {
59	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
60	  const seen = new Set<number>();
61	  let frontier: Array<[number, number]> = [[x, y]];
62	  while (frontier.length) {
63	    const next: Array<[number, number]> = [];
64	    for (const [cx, cy] of frontier) {
65	      if (cx <= 1 || cx >= st.w - 1 || cy <= 1 || cy >= st.h - 1) continue;
66	      const ii = st.idx(cx, cy);
67	      if (seen.has(ii)) continue;
68	      seen.add(ii);
69	      if (isSolidTile(st, cx, cy) || st.wall[ii] !== 0) {
70	        if (st.flags[ii]) {
71	          if (st.wall[ii] === 0) st.wall[ii] = wall;
72	          if (st.type[ii] === STONE) st.type[ii] = tile;
73	        }
74	        continue;
75	      }
76	      st.wall[ii] = wall;
77	      next.push([cx - 1, cy], [cx + 1, cy], [cx, cy - 1], [cx, cy + 1]);
78	    }
79	    frontier = next;
80	  }
81	}
82	
83	/** neonMossBiome（cs:9721-9836）1:1：随机走向游走椭圆挖苔藓洞。
84	 *  半径 num=Next(60,80)×(w/4200)、步数 num2=Next(30,40)×同系数；每步椭圆内
85	 *  （半径×(1±0.2)）暴露石头 → SpreadGrass 换霓虹苔藓；上下界钳制 + 漂移 */
86	function neonMossBiome(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number, maxY: number, neon: number): void {
87	  let vx = rng.next() * 4 - 2;
88	  let vy = rng.next() * 4 - 2;
89	  if (vx === 0) vx = 1;
90	  while (Math.hypot(vx, vy) < 4) { vx *= 1.5; vy *= 1.5; }
91	  let radius = rng.nextIntRange(60, 80);
92	  let steps = rng.nextIntRange(30, 40);
93	  const k = st.w / 4200;
94	  radius *= k;
95	  steps *= k;
96	  let px = i, py = j;
97	  while (steps > 0) {
98	    radius *= 0.98;
99	    steps -= 1;
100	    let x0 = Math.trunc(px - radius), x1 = Math.trunc(px + radius);
101	    let y0 = Math.trunc(py - radius), y1 = Math.trunc(py + radius);
102	    if (x0 < 1) x0 = 1;
103	    if (x1 > st.w - 1) x1 = st.w - 1;
104	    if (y0 < 1) y0 = 1;
105	    if (y1 > st.h - 1) y1 = st.h - 1;
106	    if (y0 < gs.rockLevel) { y0 = Math.trunc(gs.rockLevel); if (vy < 5) vy = 5; }
107	    if (y1 > maxY) { y1 = maxY; if (vy > -5) vy = -5; }
108	    const rr = radius * (1 + rng.next() * 0.4 - 0.2);
109	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
110	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
111	        const d = Math.hypot(Math.abs(x - px), Math.abs(y - py));
112	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
113	        if (d < rr * 0.8 && st.type[ii] === STONE && st.flags[ii]
114	          && (!st.flags[st.idx(x - 1, y)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x + 1, y)]
115	            || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)])) {
116	          spreadGrass(st, x - 1, y, STONE, neon, false);
117	        }
118	      }
119	    }
120	    px += vx; py += vy;
121	    vx += rng.next() * 4 - 2;
122	    vy += rng.next() * 4 - 2;
123	    vy = Math.min(10, Math.max(-10, vy));
124	    vx = Math.min(10, Math.max(-10, vx));
125	  }
126	}
127	
128	/** MossAndMossCaves pass（cs:17573-17833）1:1 */
129	export function runMossAndMossCavesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
130	  // randMoss（cs:9702-9719）：霓虹四选一；三槽 mossType 互异（0-4）
131	  const neon = NEON_TILES[rng.nextInt(NEON_TILES.length)];
132	  const mossType: number[] = [];
133	  mossType[0] = rng.nextInt(5);
134	  do { mossType[1] = rng.nextInt(5); } while (mossType[1] === mossType[0]);
135	  do { mossType[2] = rng.nextInt(5); } while (mossType[2] === mossType[0] || mossType[2] === mossType[1]);
136	
137	  // ---- 霓虹苔藓洞（cs:17583-17649）：count=w/2100 个；禁区检查 101×101 ----
138	  const caveCount = Math.floor(st.w / 2100);
139	  let placed = 0, fails = 0;
140	  while (placed < caveCount) {
141	    let x = rng.nextIntRange(100, st.w - 100);
142	    while (x > st.w * 0.38 && x < st.w * 0.62) x = rng.nextIntRange(100, st.w - 100);
143	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel + 40, gs.lavaLine - 40);
144	    let forbidden = false;
145	    outer:
146	    for (let a = x - 50; a <= x + 50; a++) {
147	      for (let b = y - 50; b <= y + 50; b++) {
148	        if (!st.inBounds(a, b)) continue;
149	        const ii = st.idx(a, b);
150	        // cs:17602-17616：仅查 active 格的 tile 族（70 砂岩/60 丛林草/367/368 蘑菇草/
151	        // 161 冰/147 雪砖/396/397 硬化沙族 + Main.tileDungeon）；墙不参与
152	        if (st.flags[ii] && (NEON_FORBIDDEN.has(st.type[ii]) || DUNGEON_TILES.has(st.type[ii]))) {
153	          forbidden = true; break outer;
154	        }
155	      }
156	    }
157	    if (forbidden) {
158	      fails++;
159	      if (fails > st.w) placed++;
160	    } else {
161	      fails = 0;
162	      placed++;
163	      neonMossBiome(st, rng, gs, x, y, gs.lavaLine, neon);
164	    }
165	  }
166	
167	  // ---- 苔藓斑（cs:17650-17713）：maxTileCount=2500 选点 → setMoss + Spread.Moss ----
168	  const countTiles = (sx: number, sy: number) => {
169	    // countTiles/nextCount（cs:9493-9560，jungle=false）：洪泛空气腔；
170	    // wall!=0 即封顶（cs:9525-9529）、墙 244/微光/岩浆封顶
171	    let air = 0, lava = 0, ice = 0, rock = 0, shroom = 0;
172	    const seen = new Set<number>();
173	    const stack: Array<[number, number]> = [[sx, sy]];
174	    const MAX = 2500;
175	    let capped = false;
176	    while (stack.length) {
177	      const [x, y] = stack.pop()!;
178	      if (air >= MAX || capped) break;
179	      if (x <= 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= st.h - 1) { capped = true; break; }
180	      const ii = st.idx(x, y);
181	      if (seen.has(ii)) continue;
182	      seen.add(ii);
183	      if (st.wall[ii] === 244) { capped = true; break; }
184	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 4) { capped = true; break; } // 微光
185	      if (st.wall[ii] !== 0) { capped = true; break; }                            // jungle=false：有墙即封顶
186	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 2) { lava++; capped = true; break; }
187	      if (st.flags[ii]) {
188	        const sh = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ii]]?.vanilla?.sheet;
189	        if (sh === 70) shroom++;
190	        if (st.type[ii] === STONE) rock++;
191	        if (sh === 147 || sh === 161) ice++;
192	      }
193	      if (!isSolidTile(st, x, y)) {
194	        air++;
195	        stack.push([x - 1, y], [x + 1, y], [x, y - 1], [x, y + 1]);
196	      }
197	    }
198	    return { air: capped ? MAX : air, lava, ice, rock, shroom };
199	  };
200	  const patches = Math.floor(st.w * 0.01);
201	  for (let n = 0; n < patches; n++) {
202	    let x = rng.nextIntRange(200, st.w - 200);
203	    let y = rng.nextIntRange(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), gs.waterLine);
204	    const nearShimmer = gs.shimmerX >= 0
205	      && Math.hypot(x - gs.shimmerX, y - gs.shimmerY) < SHIMMER_SAFETY;
206	    if (nearShimmer) continue;
207	    let ok = false;
208	    for (let tries = 0; tries < 1000; tries++) {
209	      const c = countTiles(x, y);
210	      if (c.air < 2500 && c.air >= 10 && c.lava === 0 && c.ice === 0 && c.rock > 0 && c.shroom === 0) {
211	        ok = true; break;
212	      }
213	      // cs:17698-17703 重掷 x 与 y 一起换（重掷域与初掷不同，bug-for-bug 保留）
214	      x = rng.nextIntRange(200, st.w - 200);
215	      y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel + 30, st.h - 230);
216	    }
217	    if (!ok) continue;
218	    const m = setMoss(st, x, mossType);
219	    spreadMoss(st, x, y, m.wall, m.tile);
220	  }
221	
222	  // ---- 全图散点（cs:17714-17723）：每列一次随机石头 → 苔藓 ----
223	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w; n++) {
224	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(50, st.w - 50);
225	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), gs.lavaLine);
226	    if (gs.shimmerX >= 0 && Math.hypot(x - gs.shimmerX, y - gs.shimmerY) < SHIMMER_SAFETY) continue;
227	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
228	    if (st.flags[ii] && st.type[ii] === STONE) {
229	      const m = setMoss(st, x, mossType);
230	      st.type[ii] = m.tile;
231	    }
232	  }
233	
234	  // ---- 暴露石头苔藓（cs:17725-17739）：0.05×w 个，四邻任一暴露的石头 ----
235	  //  ★ 迭代上限（原版 while 无上限）：人工构造的"无洞穴"世界（单测/迷你图）会
236	  //   永不命中暴露石头 → 死循环。真世界命中率 ≈1-5%，上限 500×budget 远超所需
237	  let budget = st.w * 0.05;
238	  for (let iter = 0; budget > 0 && iter < budget * 500; iter++) {
239	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(50, st.w - 50);
240	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), gs.lavaLine);
241	    if (gs.shimmerX >= 0 && Math.hypot(x - gs.shimmerX, y - gs.shimmerY) < SHIMMER_SAFETY) continue;
242	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
243	    if (st.flags[ii] && st.type[ii] === STONE
244	      && (!st.flags[st.idx(x - 1, y)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x + 1, y)]
245	        || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)])) {
246	      const m = setMoss(st, x, mossType);
247	      st.type[ii] = m.tile;
248	      budget -= 1;
249	    }
250	  }
251	
252	  // ---- 地狱氦苔藓 381（cs:17737-17766）：num13=w*0.065 预算 while；
253	  //  命中（25 半径框岩浆>20）-1，命中但岩浆不足 -0.002，每迭代无条件 -0.001。
254	  //  无迭代上限（原版 while(num13>0)；预算下限 0.001/迭代 → 迭代数有界）
255	  budget = st.w * 0.065;
256	  while (budget > 0) {
257	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(50, st.w - 50);
258	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.waterLine, st.h - 200);
259	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
260	    if (st.flags[ii] && st.type[ii] === STONE
261	      && (!st.flags[st.idx(x - 1, y)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x + 1, y)]
262	        || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)] || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)])) {
263	      let lava = 0;
264	      for (let a = x - 25; a < x + 25; a++) {
265	        for (let b = y - 25; b < y + 25; b++) {
266	          if (st.inBounds(a, b)) {
267	            const ji = st.idx(a, b);
268	            if (st.liquid[ji] > 0 && st.liquidType[ji] === 2) lava++;
269	          }
270	        }
271	      }
272	      if (lava > 20) {
273	        st.type[ii] = LAVA_MOSS;
274	        budget -= 1;
275	      } else {
276	        budget -= 0.002;
277	      }
278	    }
279	    budget -= 0.001;
280	  }
281	
282	  // ---- 尾段（cs:17767-17806）：全图每苔藓块四邻 grassSpread=0 的 SpreadGrass ----
283	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
284	    for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
285	      const ii = st.idx(x, y);
286	      if (!st.flags[ii]) continue;
287	      const sheet = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ii]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
288	      if (!MOSS_SHEETS.has(sheet)) continue;
289	      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
290	        const nx = k === 0 ? x - 1 : k === 1 ? x + 1 : x;
291	        const ny = k === 2 ? y - 1 : k === 3 ? y + 1 : y;
292	        // try/catch 的 repeat:false 回退对应本仓递归深度上限（无栈溢出语义，等效)
293	        spreadGrass(st, nx, ny, STONE, st.type[ii], true);
294	      }
295	    }
296	  }
297	}
298

SHEET[jt.type[i]] : -1);\n\n  // ---- ① 总量对比 ----\n  let vTotal = 0, jTotal = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {\n    if (wt.active[i] === 1 && wt.type[i] === 165) vTotal++;\n    if (jsSheet(i) === 165) jTotal++;\n  }\n  console.log(`165 总量: vanilla=${vTotal} js=${jTotal}`);\n\n  // ---- ② 双向差格分类 ----\n  const vOnly: Cell[] = [], jOnly: Cell[] = [];\n  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n    const i = y * w + x;\n    const v = wt.active[i] === 1 && wt.type[i] === 165;\n    const j = jsSheet(i) === 165;\n    if (v && !j) vOnly.push({ x, y, fx: wt.frameX[i], fy: wt.frameY[i] });\n    else if (j && !v) jOnly.push({ x, y, fx: jt.frameX[i], fy: jt.frameY[i] });\n  }\n  const famCount = (arr: Cell[]) => {\n    const m = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const c of arr) { const f = familyOf(c.fx, c.fy); m.set(f, (m.get(f) ?? 0) + 1); }\n    return [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);\n  };\n  console.log(`vOnly=${vOnly.length} jOnly=${jOnly.length}`);\n  console.log('vOnly 族列分布:', famCount(vOnly).slice(0, 12));\n  console.log('jOnly 族列分布:', famCount(jOnly).slice(0, 12));\n\n  // ---- ③ 邻域签名（原版侧）：差格的墙 + 上下邻 tile ----\n  const nbhd = (arr: Cell[]) => {\n    const wallC = new Map<number, number>();\n    const upC = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const c of arr) {\n      const i = c.y * w + c.x;\n      wallC.set(wt.wall[i], (wallC.get(wt.wall[i]) ?? 0) + 1);\n      if (c.y > 0) {\n        const u = wt.active[i - w] === 1 ? String(wt.type[i - w]) : 'air';\n        upC.set(u, (upC.get(u) ?? 0) + 1);\n      }\n    }\n    const top = (m: Map<string | number, number>, n: number) => [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, n);\n    return { wall: top(wallC, 8), up: top(upC, 8) };\n  };\n  console.log('vOnly 邻域(wall/up):', JSON.stringify(nbhd(vOnly)));\n  console.log('jOnly 邻域(wall/up):', JSON.stringify(nbhd(jOnly)));\n\n  // ---- ④ 全量 165（含两侧都有但帧不同）帧对比 ----\n  const bothFrameDiff = { n: 0, col: new Map<string, number>() };\n  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {\n    const v = wt.active[i] === 1 && wt.type[i] === 165;\n    const j = jsSheet(i) === 165;\n    if (v && j && (wt.frameX[i] !== jt.frameX[i] || wt.frameY[i] !== jt.frameY[i])) {\n      bothFrameDiff.n++;\n      const k = `${wt.frameX[i]},${wt.frameY[i]}>${jt.frameX[i]},${jt.frameY[i]}`;\n      bothFrameDiff.col.set(k, (bothFrameDiff.col.get(k) ?? 0) + 1);\n    }\n  }\n  console.log(`两侧都有但帧不同: n=${bothFrameDiff.n}`,\n    [...bothFrameDiff.col.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6));\n\n  // ---- ⑤ 空间聚类粗报：vOnly/jOnly 按 64 格桶列分布（找聚集带）----\n  const bucket = (arr: Cell[]) => {\n    const m = new Map<number, number>();\n    for (const c of arr) { const b = (c.x >> 6) << 6; m.set(b, (m.get(b) ?? 0) + 1); }\n    return [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 10);\n  };\n  console.log('vOnly 热桶(x>>6):', bucket(vOnly));\n  console.log('jOnly 热桶(x>>6):', bucket(jOnly));\n\n  writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify({\n    file: FILE, vTotal, jTotal, vOnlyN: vOnly.length, jOnlyN: jOnly.length,\n    vOnlyFam: famCount(vOnly), jOnlyFam: famCount(jOnly),\n    vOnlySample: vOnly.slice(0, 400), jOnlySample: jOnly.slice(0, 400),\n  }));\n  console.log(`详情样本已写 ${OUT}`);\n}, 240_000);\n"
}

42	const HARDSAND = sid(397);
43	const SHELLPILE = sid(495);     // (b) k+1/k+2 停止条件额外容许的贝壳堆
44	const MUD = sid(59);
45	const SILT = sid(123);
46	const SLUSH = sid(224);
47	const MARBLE = sid(367);
48	const GRANITE = sid(368);
49	const HIVE = sid(225);          // (e) 半砖豁免档（cs:18078）
50	const TRAP = sid(137);          // (e) 邻格机关陷阱 → 清除（cs:18084/18096）
51	
52	const WALL_SANDSTONE = 187;     // WallID.Sandstone（地下沙漠天然沙岩墙）
53	const WALL_HARDSAND = 216;      // WallID.HardenedSand（天然硬化沙墙）
54	
55	const NON_SOLID_SAVE_SLOPES = idsByVanillaSheet(131, 351, 336, 340, 341, 342, 343, 344);
56	const PLATFORMS = idsByVanillaSheet(19, 427, 435, 436, 437, 438, 439);
57	
58	/** SaveSlopes[t] = tileSolid[t]（含 pass 头 137/130 翻转）∪ NonSolidSaveSlopes */
59	function isSaveSlope(t: number): boolean {
60	  return (genSolidType(t) && !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) || NON_SOLID_SAVE_SLOPES.has(t);
61	}
62	
63	/** WorldGen.SolidTile(cs:42370)：active && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && !halfBrick
64	 *  && slope==0（tileSolidTop 以 platform 近似=TileCleanupPass 同口径） */
65	function solidTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
66	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
67	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
68	  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
69	  const t = st.type[i];
70	  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
71	  return genSolidType(t) && !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0;
72	}
73	
74	/** oceanDepths(cs:10978-10989)：y 超海洋层 false；横向进沙滩带 true
75	 *  （与 CorruptionPass/FinalCleanupPass 同式，oceanLevel=(ws+rl)/2+40 cs:4393） */
76	function oceanDepths(st: TileStore, gs: GenState, x: number, y: number): boolean {
77	  if (y > (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40) return false;
78	  return x < gs.beachDistance || x > st.w - gs.beachDistance;
79	}
80	
81	export function runQuickCleanupPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
82	  // ★pass 头翻转（cs:17997-17998：机关陷阱 137/活性石 130 非实心），尾 cs:18104-05 还原
83	  flipGenSolid([137, 130], false);
84	  const { w, h } = st;
85	  for (let i = 20; i < w - 20; i++) {
86	    for (let j = 20; j < h - 20; j++) {
87	      const t = st.idx(i, j);
88	
89	      // (a) 海洋带液体转水（cs:18005-18008）
90	      if (oceanDepths(st, gs, i, j) && st.liquid[t] > 0 && st.liquidType[t] !== LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) {
91	        st.liquidType[t] = LIQUID_TYPE.WATER;
92	      }
93	
94	      // (b) 海洋带表面沙下方填土（cs:18009-18022）。Next(4,7) 在条件里=逐判定消耗
95	      if (j < gs.worldSurface && oceanDepths(st, gs, i, j) && st.flags[t] === 1 && st.type[t] === SAND) {
96	        if (st.slope[t] === 3 || st.slope[t] === 4) st.slope[t] = 0; // bottomSlope → slope(0)
97	        for (
98	          let k = j + 1;
99	          k < j + rng.int(4, 6)
100	            && (!st.flags[st.idx(i, k)] || (st.type[st.idx(i, k)] !== HARDSAND && st.type[st.idx(i, k)] !== SAND))
101	            && (!st.flags[st.idx(i, k + 1)] || (st.type[st.idx(i, k + 1)] !== HARDSAND && st.type[st.idx(i, k + 1)] !== SAND && st.type[st.idx(i, k + 1)] !== SHELLPILE))
102	            && (!st.flags[st.idx(i, k + 2)] || (st.type[st.idx(i, k + 2)] !== HARDSAND && st.type[st.idx(i, k + 2)] !== SAND && st.type[st.idx(i, k + 2)] !== SHELLPILE));
103	          k++
104	        ) {
105	          st.setTileSilent(i, k, DIRT);
106	        }
107	      }
108	
109	      // (c) 沙岩/硬化沙墙内杂块转硬化沙 + 液体二分（cs:18023-18045，不查 active）
110	      if (st.wall[t] === WALL_SANDSTONE || st.wall[t] === WALL_HARDSAND) {
111	        if (st.type[t] === MUD || st.type[t] === SILT || st.type[t] === SLUSH) st.type[t] = HARDSAND;
112	        if (st.type[t] === GRANITE || st.type[t] === MARBLE) st.type[t] = HARDSAND;
113	        if (j <= gs.rockLevel) {
114	          st.liquid[t] = 0;
115	        } else if (st.liquid[t] > 0) {
116	          st.liquid[t] = 255;
117	          st.liquidType[t] = LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA;
118	        }
119	      }
120	
121	      // (d) 表面悬空沙下墙补齐（cs:18046-18069）。★break 只断内层 m 循环——
122	      //     num 最终=从左到右各列首个墙值的最后覆盖（bug-for-bug 保留）
123	      if (j < gs.worldSurface && st.flags[t] === 1 && st.type[t] === SAND
124	        && st.wall[st.idx(i, j + 1)] === 0 && !solidTile(st, i, j + 1)) {
125	        let num = 0;
126	        for (let l = i - 3; l <= i + 3; l++) {
127	          for (let m = j - 3; m <= j + 3; m++) {
128	            const wm = st.wall[st.idx(l, m)];
129	            if (wm > 0) {
130	              num = wm;
131	              break; // 仅断 m 循环（原版 cs:18058 同）
132	            }
133	          }
134	        }
135	        if (num > 0) {
136	          st.wall[st.idx(i, j + 1)] = num;
137	          if (st.wall[t] === 0) st.wall[t] = num;
138	        }
139	      }
140	
141	      // (e) SaveSlopes 坡面/半砖归一（cs:18070-18101）
142	      if (!st.flags[t] || !isSaveSlope(st.type[t])) {
143	        st.slope[t] = 0;
144	        st.half[t] = 0; // ClearSlope = slope(0)+halfBrick(false)（Tile.cs:158-163）
145	      } else if (!PLATFORMS.has(st.type[t]) && !NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[t])) {
146	        const topSlope = st.slope[t] === 1 || st.slope[t] === 2;
147	        const bottomSlope = st.slope[t] === 3 || st.slope[t] === 4;
148	        if (topSlope || st.half[t] === 1) {
149	          if (st.type[t] !== HIVE || st.half[t] === 0) {

151	            if (st.type[st.idx(i + 1, j)] === TRAP || st.type[st.idx(i - 1, j)] === TRAP) st.setTileSilent(i, j, 0);
152	          }
153	        } else if (bottomSlope) {
154	          if (!solidTile(st, i, j - 1)) st.setTileSilent(i, j, 0);
155	          if (st.type[st.idx(i + 1, j)] === TRAP || st.type[st.idx(i - 1, j)] === TRAP) st.setTileSilent(i, j, 0);
156	        }
157	      }
158	    }
159	  }
160	  // pass 尾还原（cs:18104-18105）
161	  flipGenSolid([137, 130], true);
162	}

function countedRng(seed: number) {
  const rng = new RNG(seed);
  const ur = (rng as unknown as { ur: { internalSample: () => number } }).ur;
  const orig = ur.internalSample.bind(ur);
  const box = { n: 0 };
  ur.internalSample = () => { box.n++; return orig(); };
  return { rng, box };
}

function cloneGs(gs: GenState): GenState {
  const c = { ...gs } as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const k of Object.keys(c)) {
    const v = c[k];
    if (Array.isArray(v)) c[k] = v.map((e) => (e && typeof e === 'object' ? { ...(e as object) } : e));
    else if (v instanceof Int32Array) c[k] = v.slice();
    else if (v && typeof v === 'object') c[k] = { ...(v as Record<string, unknown>) };
  }
  return c as unknown as GenState;
}

	private GenPassResult RunPass(GenPass pass)
	{
		if (!pass.Enabled)
		{
			return new GenPassResult
			{
				Name = pass.Name,
				Skipped = true
			};
		}
		Stopwatch stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
		Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed);
		_progress.Start(pass.Weight);
		try
		{
			pass.Apply(_progress, _configuration.GetPassConfiguration(pass.Name));
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			_controller.ReportException("Exception in Pass: " + pass.Name, ex);
		}
		_progress.End();
		return new GenPassResult
		{
			Name = pass.Name,
			DurationMs = (int)stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds,
			RandNext = WorldGen.genRand.Next()
		};
	}

function vanillaTerrain(ctx: GenCtx) {
  const { store, world, surface, cfg } = ctx;
  let rng = ctx.rng;
  const gs = newGenState(store.w, store.h);
  // 世界种子 int——DesertHive 的 FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(57005) 主种子
  // （DesertHive.cs:253，与 genRand 完全独立的第二条随机流）
  gs.seed = translateSeed(cfg.seedText);
  // 彩蛋种子旗标（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed）——特殊种子 pass 分支消费（GemPasses 等）；
  // 金标种子 flags 恒空集 → 掷骰流位置不受影响
  gs.seedFlags = parseSeed(cfg.seedText).flags;
  // ===== Reset 掷骰（1456 WorldGen.generateWorld cs:11159-11472 逐行 1:1）=====
  // 原版在 AddPasses 循环之前按此固定顺序消耗 Main.rand（种子等价的流位置契约）。
  // 天气/背景/月亮等值我们未消费——照掷照弃，只保留 GenState 下游字段。
  const W = store.w;
  // ① jungleHut（cs:11182 Next(5)，cs:11333-11352 映射 0-4→119/120/158/175/45）
  //    JungleShrinePass 复用本值（曾照掷照弃致 pass 内重掷、流位+样式双偏）
  gs.jungleHut = rng.nextInt(5);
  // ② crimsonLeft（cs:11235）——邪恶群系左右分布，未消费
  rng.nextInt(2);
  // ③ RandomizeWeather（cs:7736-7746）：numClouds + 风速 do-while
  rng.nextIntRange(10, 200);
  for (;;) {
    const wf = rng.next();              // NextFloat = (float)NextDouble（1 次采样）
    rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);             // Next(2)（符号）
    if (wf !== 0) break;                // ×0.35×(±1) 非零 ⇔ wf≠0
  }
  // ④ 地狱箱主件洗牌（cs:11262-11274）：Next(5..1) 逐个抽出 → gs.hellChestItems
  {
    const pool = [274, 220, 112, 218, 3019];
    const out: number[] = [];
    while (pool.length > 0) out.push(...pool.splice(rng.nextInt(pool.length), 1));
    // 多格残片清扫(本仓补,原版由 KillTile 整块语义天然保证):破损的可放置
    // 多格物体(陶罐/雕像/碗/起爆器/生命水晶)整体移除;祭坛/装饰组/海燕麦
    // 为原版保留形态,不在清扫列(集成 FRAGMENTS 白名单在案)。零掷骰
    { name: '多格残片清扫', run: multitileSweepPass },
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < passes.length; i++) {
    onProgress?.(passes[i].name, i / passes.length);
    await nextFrame();
    // ★RunPass 种子重播（1456 WorldGenerator.cs:497：每个 GenPass 执行前
    //   Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed)，IL 实证）——槽头重播保证"每槽
    //   至少以新流开始"；槽内含多个原版 pass 的包装（vanillaCaves/vanillaBiomes/
    //   vanillaStructures/statuesChestsPass 等）另有内部各 pass 边界的 rng.reseed。
    ctx.rng.reseed(world.seed);
    await passes[i].run(ctx, () => {});
    // 部分世界回调（生成期实时预览）：pass 完成即暴露，不等 finalize
    if (cfg.onWorldPartial) cfg.onWorldPartial(world, i, passes[i].name);
  }

  finalize(ctx);
  // ★终扫(finalize 之后):finalize 的幽灵 type 净化会把"flags=0 但 type 残留"
  // 的多格物体清成 0 → 在清扫 pass 之后【再造残片】(2026-08-13 缺位断言实锤:
  // 生成后 pot 残片 1 处,补扫即清零)。清扫幂等且廉价,此处兜底。
  runMultitileFragmentSweepPass(ctx.store);
  onProgress?.('完成', 1);
  return world;

{
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tools/_165bnd.test.ts",
 "content": "// 165 族槽位边界对账（env 门控=SW_165B=1）：4200×1200 seed 9293480（dump 同参），\n// 在与原版 dump 对齐的槽边界抓全量 state，对拍 typ/act/wal 三通道 + 165 双向差。\n// 目标：找 D(输出)−D(输入) 跳变最大的槽 = 165 漂移的其余贡献 pass。\nimport { it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\n\nconst RUN = process.env.SW_165B === '1';\nconst VG = '/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g';\nconst W = 4200, H = 1200;\nconst SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);\nfor (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;\n}\n/** 槽号 → 对齐 dump（该槽【完成后】的原版边界） */\nconst ALIGN: Array<[number, number, string]> = [\n  [18, 63, 'Spider_Caves'],\n  [19, 64, 'Gem_Caves'],\n  [20, 65, 'Moss'],\n  [24, 69, 'Floating_Island_Houses'],\n  [25, 70, 'Quick_Cleanup'],\n  [26, 71, 'Pots'],\n  [39, 85, 'Webs_And_Honey'],\n  [44, 91, 'Mushrooms'],\n  [45, 93, 'Random_Gems'],\n  [46, 94, 'Moss_Grass'],\n  [48, 96, 'Larva'],\n  [51, 99, 'Tile_Cleanup'],\n  [53, 101, 'Micro_Biomes'],\n  [54, 102, 'Water_Plants'],\n  [55, 103, 'Stalac'],\n  [56, 104, 'Remove_Broken_Traps'],\n  [57, 105, 'Final_Cleanup'],\n];\nconst ALIGN_MAP = new Map(ALIGN.map(([slot, idx, name]) => [slot, { idx, name }]));\n\nfunction diffBoundary(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array }, idx: number, name: string, label: string) {\n  const vt = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${VG}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${name}.typ`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const va = readFileSync(`${VG}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${name}.act`);\n  const vw = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${VG}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${name}.wal`).buffer.slice(0));\n  let typ = 0, act = 0, wal = 0, v165 = 0, j165 = 0;\n  const pairM = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {\n    const ja = st.flags[i] !== 0;\n    const jt = ja ? SHEET[st.type[i]] : -1;\n    const vAc = va[i] !== 0;\n    const vt2 = vAc ? vt[i] : -1;\n    if (jt === -2) continue; // unmapped（不应出现）\n    if (vAc !== ja) act++;\n    if (vt2 !== jt) { typ++; const k = `${vt2}>${jt}`; pairM.set(k, (pairM.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); }\n    if (vw[i] !== st.wall[i]) wal++;\n    if (vt2 === 165 && jt !== 165) v165++;\n    if (jt === 165 && vt2 !== 165) j165++;\n  }\n  const top = [...pairM.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6);\n  console.log(`${label} → dump${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${name}: typ=${typ} act=${act} wal=${wal} | v165only=${v165} j165only=${j165} | top=${JSON.stringify(top)}`);\n  return { typ, act, wal, v165, j165 };\n}\n\n(RUN ? it : it.skip)('aligned slot boundary diff', async () => {\n  let prev: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array } | null = null;\n  const results: Array<{ label: string; d: ReturnType<typeof diffBoundary> }> = [];\n  await generateWorld({\n    width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,\n    onWorldPartial: (w, i, name) => {\n      const al = ALIGN_MAP.get(i);\n      const st = w.store;\n      if (al) {\n        const label = `slot${String(i).padStart(2)}_${name}`;\n        results.push({ label, d: diffBoundary({ type: st.type, flags: st.flags, wall: st.wall }, al.idx, al.name, label) });\n      }\n      // 前一快照（首个对齐槽的【输入】基线）：在首个对齐槽开始前抓一次\n      if (i === 17 && !prev) {\n        prev = { type: st.type.slice(), flags: st.flags.slice(), wall: st.wall.slice() };\n      }\n    },\n  });\n  if (prev) results.unshift({ label: 'slot18_in(≈dump062)', d: diffBoundary(prev, 62, 'Water_Chests', 'slot18_in') });\n  console.log('—— 汇总（typ 差 / v165only / j165only）——');\n  for (const r of results) console.log(`${r.label.padEnd(24)} typ=${String(r.d.typ).padStart(6)} 165v=${String(r.d.v165).padStart(5)} 165j=${String(r.d.j165).padStart(5)}`);\n}, 600_000);\n"
}

	public static void PlaceTight(int x, int y, bool spiders = false)
	{
		if (Main.tile[x, y - 1] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y - 1] = new Tile();
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y] = new Tile();
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y + 1] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y + 1] = new Tile();
		}
		if (!Main.tile[x, y].anyShimmer() && (!Main.tile[x, y].active() || Main.tile[x, y].type != 231))
		{
			PlaceUncheckedStalactite(x, y, genRand.Next(2) == 0, genRand.Next(3), spiders);
			if (Main.tile[x, y].active() && Main.tile[x, y].type == 165)
			{
				CheckStalactite(x, y);
			}
		}
	}

	public static void PlaceUncheckedStalactite(int x, int y, bool preferSmall, int variation, bool spiders)
	{
		ushort type = 165;
		variation = Utils.Clamp(variation, 0, 2);

```
Vanilla PlaceUncheckedStalactite matches the JS placeUncheckedInitial (VV's port) — I verified the family columns: ice family (147/161/163/164/200) → 0-base; stone family (1/moss/117/25/203) → 54-base; 225 → 162; 396/397 → 378; 368 → 432; 367 → 486. Bottom branch: same families minus ice (no ice in bottom!). JS matches: bottom has 1/moss/117/25/203, 225, 396/397, 368, 367 — NO ice family ✓ (JS placeUncheckedInitial bottom section also lacks ice ✓).

Wait — one subtle thing in vanilla top branch: the checks are SEPARATE `if` blocks (not else-if): ice-family block, then stone-family block, then 225, 396/397, 368, 367 — all can fire?? A type can only be in one family, so effectively exclusive. JS uses separate ifs too ✓.

Also the top branch requires `SolidTile(x, y-1) && !active(x,y) && !active(x,y+1)` — JS: `if (solidTileAt(st, x, y - 1) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)])` ✓.

Bottom branch: `spiders || !SolidTile(x, y+1) || active(x,y) || active(x,y-1)` → return. JS: `if (!solidTileAt(st, x, y + 1) || st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] || st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)]) return;` ✓ (spiders=false always here ✓).

BUT — CRITICAL: the family checks read `Main.tile[x, y-1].type` REGARDLESS of active-ness. JS `sheetOf` returns -1 if !flags → returns sheet only if active. Vanilla reads .type even for inactive tiles (ghost types!). During worldgen, can (x,y-1) be inactive with a ghost type that matches a family (e.g., 1)? The gate requires SolidTile(x,y-1) which requires active() — so (x,y-1) IS active in this branch. ✓ same.

For the bottom branch: SolidTile(x,y+1) required → active ✓.

export function checkStalactite(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, j: number): void {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return;
  const i0 = st.idx(x, j);
  if (!st.flags[i0]) return;
  const fy = st.frameY[i0]!;
  if (fy === 72) {
    let flag = !solidTileSlope(st, x, j - 1);
    if (!flag && !updateStalagtiteStyle(st, rng, x, j)) flag = true;
    if (flag) killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, j);
    return;
  }
  if (fy === 90) {
    let flag2 = !solidTileSlope(st, x, j + 1);
    // InvalidTileForPilesOrSpeleothems = TileID.Sets.Boulders（生成期此阶段无巨石）
    if (!flag2 && !updateStalagtiteStyle(st, rng, x, j)) flag2 = true;
    if (flag2) killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, j);
    return;
  }
  if (fy >= 36) {
    let num = j;
    if (st.frameY[i0] === 54) num--;
    let flag3 = !solidTileSlope(st, x, num + 2);
    const a = st.inBounds(x, num) ? st.idx(x, num) : -1;
    const b = st.inBounds(x, num + 1) ? st.idx(x, num + 1) : -1;
    if (a < 0 || b < 0) { killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, num); return; }
    if (!st.flags[a] || !st.flags[b] || st.type[b] !== st.type[a]) flag3 = true;
    if (st.frameX[b]! !== st.frameX[a]!) flag3 = true;
    if (!flag3 && !updateStalagtiteStyle(st, rng, x, num)) flag3 = true;
    // ★原版击杀带类型门（cs:39146-39155/39193-39202）：仅 type == 被查格（165）的
    // 格被 KillTile——num/num+1 被后续放置覆写成大理石等非 165 时**不得连带击杀**
    // （曾无条件双双击杀 → 刚放的板格被抹 → SmoothSlope/SolidTile 门级联分叉，
    // biome2 slab78 van48/js63 掷混合分歧根因）
    if (flag3) {
      if (st.type[a] === st.type[i0]) killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, num);
      if (st.type[b] === st.type[i0]) killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, num + 1);
    }
    return;
  }
  // 垂挂双格（frameY 0/18）
  let num = j;
  if (st.frameY[i0] === 18) num--;
  let flag4 = !solidTileSlope(st, x, num - 1);
  const a = st.inBounds(x, num) ? st.idx(x, num) : -1;
  const b = st.inBounds(x, num + 1) ? st.idx(x, num + 1) : -1;
  if (a < 0 || b < 0) { killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, num); return; }
  if (!st.flags[a] || !st.flags[b] || st.type[b] !== st.type[a]) flag4 = true;
  if (st.frameX[b]! !== st.frameX[a]!) flag4 = true;
  if (!flag4 && !updateStalagtiteStyle(st, rng, x, num)) flag4 = true;
  // ★同上：类型门击杀（cs:39193-39202）——非 165 占位格不连带
  if (flag4) {

    if (st.type[b] === st.type[i0]) killStalactiteTile(st, rng, x, num + 1);
  }
}

	public static bool TryGrowingTreeByType(int treeTileType, int checkedX, int checkedY, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		bool result = false;
		switch (treeTileType)
		{
		case 5:
			result = GrowTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 323:
			result = GrowPalmTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 587:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Ruby, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 588:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Diamond, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 583:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Topaz, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 584:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amethyst, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 589:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amber, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 586:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Emerald, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 585:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Sapphire, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 596:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Sakura, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 616:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Willow, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 634:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static bool GrowTreeWithSettings(int checkedX, int checkedY, GrowTreeSettings settings, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		int num = checkedY;
		while (Main.tile[checkedX, checkedY].active() && Main.tile[checkedX, num].type == settings.SaplingTileType)
		{
			num++;
		}
		if (Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num - 1].liquid != 0 || Main.tile[checkedX, num - 1].liquid != 0 || Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num - 1].liquid != 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[checkedX, num];
		if (!tile.nactive() || tile.halfBrick() || tile.slope() != 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool flag = ignoreWalls || settings.WallTest(Main.tile[checkedX, num - 1].wall);
		if (!settings.GroundTest(tile.type) || !flag)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if ((!Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num].active() || !settings.GroundTest(Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num].type)) && (!Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num].active() || !settings.GroundTest(Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num].type)))
		{
			return false;
		}
		TileColorCache cache = Main.tile[checkedX, num].BlockColorAndCoating();

511:export function growGemTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, treeTileType: number): boolean {
export function growGemTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, treeTileType: number): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, { ...GEM_PROFILE, treeTile: treeTileType });
}

/** 观赏树（TryGrowingTreeByType 616 柳 / 596 樱 → VanityTree_Willow/Sakura profile，cs:3872-3894） */
export function growVanityTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, willow: boolean): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, willow ? WILLOW_PROFILE : SAKURA_PROFILE);
}

/** 灰烬树（TryGrowingTreeByType 634 → GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash，cs:3894-3903/
 *  30538）：灰烬草 633 基座、高 7-12。地狱 pass 铺 633 后 1/3 概率逐点尝试（cs:13885） */
export function growAshTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, ASH_PROFILE);
}

/** AddTrees（1456 cs:31523-31578）1:1：逐列尝试（~1.6 列间距）；沙滩棕榈按每侧固定概率；
 *  草上 1/20 观赏树（type==2 且 Next(20)==0 → Next(2)==0 ? 柳 616 : 樱 596），否则普通树 5 */
export function runTreePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  // EpicTree 林区（pass 82 头段，L9656-9666）：w*0.003 个 25-50 半宽林区逐格尝试
  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * 0.003; n++) {
    const cx = rng.int(50, st.w - 51);            // Next(50, maxTilesX-50)
    const hw = rng.int(25, 49);                  // Next(25, 50)
    for (let x = cx - hw; x < cx + hw; x++) {
      for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) growEpicTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
    }
  }
  const palmL = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;          // 每侧棕榈概率（L15644-45）
  const palmR = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;
  const GRASS_TILE = T.GRASS;                     // tile 2（vanilla 草）
  for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
    for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
      if (x < 380) {
        if (rng.next() < palmL && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y, gs);
      } else if (x > st.w - 380) {
        if (rng.next() < palmR && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y, gs);
      }
      // 1/20 观赏树（cs:31558-31566）：active 且 type==2 且 Next(20)==0 → 柳/樱对半
      const yi = st.idx(x, y);
      if (st.flags[yi] && st.type[yi] === GRASS_TILE && rng.int(0, 19) === 0) {
        growVanityTree(st, rng, x, y, rng.int(0, 1) === 0);
        continue;
      }
      growTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
    }
    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) x++;                 // Next(3)==0 跳列
    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) x++;                 // Next(4)==0 跳列
  }
}

/** RandomizeTreeStyle（L2817）：按世界宽掷 treeX 边界与 treeStyle（互异，style0→4 概率 2/3）。
 *  Reset pass 调用（L4837，worldID 之后 dungeonSide 之前） */
export function randomizeTreeStyle(rng: RNG, w: number): { treeX: number[]; treeStyle: number[] } {
  const treeX = [0, 0, 0], treeStyle = [0, 0, 0, 0];
  const roll6 = () => rng.int(0, 5);
  // fix0 原位改写 treeStyle 前 count 位（cs:8095-8101 style0 → 4，2/3 概率）。
  // ★曾 fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1]]) 传入临时拷贝——掷骰照旧（Next(3) 无条件
  //   消耗）但翻转落在被丢弃的副本上，4200/6400 档 style0 从未被替换成 4
  //   （.wld 头 treeStyle 对账实锤，2026-08-16 多尺寸批修复）
  const fix0 = (arr: number[], count: number) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      if (arr[i] === 0 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) arr[i] = 4; // style0 → 4（2/3）
    }
  };
  if (w === 4200) {
    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.25), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.25) - 1);
    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
    treeX[1] = w; treeX[2] = w;
    fix0(treeStyle, 2);
  } else if (w === 6400) {
    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.334 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.334 + w * 0.2) - 1);
    treeX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.667 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.667 + w * 0.2) - 1);
    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6(); treeStyle[2] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[1]) treeStyle[2] = roll6();
    treeX[2] = w;
    fix0(treeStyle, 3);
  } else {
    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.25 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.25 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    treeX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    treeX[2] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.75 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.75 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6(); treeStyle[2] = roll6(); treeStyle[3] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[1]) treeStyle[2] = roll6();
    while (treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[1] || treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[2]) treeStyle[3] = roll6();
    fix0(treeStyle, 4);
  }
  return { treeX, treeStyle };
}

/** RandomizeCaveBackgrounds（1456 WorldGen.cs:8157-8210）：按世界宽掷 caveBackX 边界与

export function growTreeWithSettings(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, p: TreeProfile): boolean {

  // 越过树苗找地面行（cs:30547-30550）——★active() 读的是**原始 checkedY** 行
  //  （原版反编译实况），type 读推进中的 num 行
  let num = y;
  while (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && st.inBounds(x, num) && st.type[st.idx(x, num)] === p.sapling) num++;
  if (!st.inBounds(x, num) || num < 1) return false;
  // 基座上一格三列无液体（cs:30551-30554）
  if (st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, num - 1)] !== 0) return false;
  // 基座整砖 + 墙白名单 + GroundTest（cs:30555-30566）
  const gi = st.idx(x, num);
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0) return false;
  if (!p.walls.has(st.wall[st.idx(x, num - 1)])) return false;
  if (!p.ground.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  // 两侧至少一侧同基座（cs:30568-30571）
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(x + dx, num)
    && st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, num)] && p.ground.has(st.type[st.idx(x + dx, num)]);
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
  const height = rng.int(p.heightMin, p.heightMax);    // Next(TreeHeightMin, TreeHeightMax+1)
  const num4 = height + p.topPadding;                  // + TreeTopPaddingNeeded
  // EmptyTileCheck(x-2, x+2, num-num4, num-1, 20)（cs:30580-30582）——统一走
  // emptyTileCheck 忠实语义（比旧 GEM_EMPTY_ALLOW 表多容忍 CommonSapling 族）
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, x - 2, x + 2, num - num4, num - 1, 20)) return false;
  // 干身帧循环（cs:30590-30816）：与 GrowTree 同表，tile 换成宝石树干。
  // ★掷序与 GrowTree 相同（cs:30595-30596）：先 num5=Next(3)（干身变体）再
  //   num6=Next(10)（枝型）——旧实现曾 num6 先掷致流值互换（shimmer 金标
  //   2026-08-13 暴露：成树 x=3865 reroll 2 vs 1）
  let flagL = false, flagR = false;
  for (let i = num - height; i < num; i++) {
    const num5 = rng.int(0, 2);
    let num6 = rng.int(0, 9);
    if (i === num - 1 || i === num - height) num6 = 0; // 顶/底强制直干
    while (((num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) && flagL) || ((num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) && flagR)) { num6 = rng.int(0, 9); }
    flagL = false; flagR = false;
    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) flagL = true;
    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) flagR = true;
    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num6][num5];
    st.setTileSilent(x, i, p.treeTile, fx, fy);
    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) {                    // 左枝（cs:30741-30764）
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(x - 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
    }
    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) {                    // 右枝（cs:30766-30789）
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(x + 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
    }
  }
  // 基座 tuft（cs:30819-30858）：两侧地面适树（草族）才出，各 1/3 概率抹除；
  // 以太腔地面是石头 → IsTileTypeFitForTree=false，天然全跳过（1:1 保留判定）
  const tuftFit = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, num)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(x + dx, num);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && GEM_TREE_FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  let tL = tuftFit(-1), tR = tuftFit(1);
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tL = false;
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tR = false;
  if (tR) st.setTileSilent(x + 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 22, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  if (tL) st.setTileSilent(x - 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 44, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  // ★cs:30906 `num5 = genRand.Next(3)` 是**无条件掷**（基座干身帧骰，帧写才受
  //   flag4/flag5 门控 cs:30907-30959）。旧实现内嵌 `if (tL || tR)`——两侧 tuft 全
  //   false 时（以太腔石地恒如此）每棵成树少掷 1 骰，500 采样流整体漂移
  //   （shimmer 金标 2026-08-13 暴露：CS oracle 状态恢复对账 tree[41] 后分叉）
  const baseK = rng.int(0, 2);
  if (tL && tR) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 88, 132 + baseK * 22);  // cs:30907-30923
  else if (tL) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 0, 132 + baseK * 22);    // cs:30925-30941
  else if (tR) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 66, 132 + baseK * 22);   // cs:30943-30959
  // 树冠标记帧（cs:30932-30967）：12/13 fx=22 否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k
  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;
  st.setTileSilent(x, num - height, p.treeTile, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  return true;
}

/** 宝石树（TryGrowingTreeByType 583-589 → GemTree_* profile）。
 *  treeTileType = 583-589（黄玉/紫晶/蓝玉/翡翠/红宝石/钻石/琥珀，cs:35063-35079 num24 映射） */
export function growGemTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, treeTileType: number): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, { ...GEM_PROFILE, treeTile: treeTileType });
}

 "new_string": "    const rng2 = new RNG(cap.gs.seed);\n    // 手工同构扫描（语义拷贝自 GemPasses/TreePass），树用真 growGemTree 保门与掷全同构\n    const gs = cap.gs;\n    const f = gs.seedFlags ?? {};\n    const alwaysTry = !!(f.tenthAnniversary || f.drunkWorld);\n    const oceanDepths = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n      if (y > (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40) return false;\n      return x < gs.beachDistance || x > W - gs.beachDistance;\n    };\n    let placed22671 = false;\n    let trees = 0;\n    for (let x = 20; x <= LOG_X; x++) {\n      for (let y = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface); y < H - 20; y++) {\n        const treeRoll = alwaysTry ? true : rng2.nextInt(5) === 0;\n        if (treeRoll && rs2.liquid[rs2.idx(x, y - 1)] === 0) {\n          const treeType = 583 + rng2.nextInt(7);\n          if (growGemTree(rs2, rng2, x, y, treeType)) trees++;\n        }\n        if (!oceanDepths(x, y) && !rs2.flags[rs2.idx(x, y)] && rng2.nextInt(5) === 0) {\n          if (x === 22 && y === 671) placed22671 = true;\n          // placeTightInitial 语义（Next(2)/Next(3) 恒掷；放置门由 dump 输入决定）\n          rng2.nextInt(2); rng2.nextInt(3);\n        }\n      }\n      for (let k = 5; k < gs.worldSurface; k++) {\n        const upA = rs2.flags[rs2.idx(x, k - 1)] ? SHEET[rs2.type[rs2.idx(x, k - 1)]] : -1;\n        const dnA = rs2.flags[rs2.idx(x, k + 1)] ? SHEET[rs2.type[rs2.idx(x, k + 1)]] : -1;\n        if ((upA === 147 || upA === 161) && rng2.nextInt(5) === 0) { rng2.nextInt(2); rng2.nextInt(3); }\n        if ((upA === 25 || upA === 203) && rng2.nextInt(5) === 0) { rng2.nextInt(2); rng2.nextInt(3); }\n        if ((dnA === 25 || dnA === 203) && rng2.nextInt(5) === 0) { rng2.nextInt(2); rng2.nextInt(3); }\n      }\n    }\n    console.log(`列段[20,23] fresh 流: (22,671) roll==0 → ${placed22671}; 成树=${trees}`);"
}
```

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-17T17:02:15.225Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
  private ur: UnifiedRandom;
  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
  }

  next(): number {
    return this.ur.nextDouble();
  }

  /** 闭区间 [min, max]，≡ 原版 Next(min, max+1) */
  int(min: number, max: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(min, max + 1);
  }

  range(min: number, max: number): number {
    return min + this.ur.nextDouble() * (max - min);
  }

  chance(p: number): boolean {
    return this.ur.nextDouble() < p;
  }

  pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
    return arr[this.ur.nextInt(arr.length)];
  }

  /** 原版裸方法直通（Reset 序列/pass 级对账用，语义与 C# 完全一致） */
  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextInt(maxValue);
  }

  /** RunPass 种子重播（1456 WorldGenerator.cs:497：每个 GenPass 执行前
   *  `Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed)`，IL 实证 newobj+stsfld Main::rand；
   *  GenBase._random => genRand => Main.rand ⇒ 各 pass 掷骰流均从 stream(seed)
   *  第 0 掷开始，pass 间不续流）。原地重置内部流，保持对象引用——ctx.rng 与
   *  闭包捕获在重播后仍然有效。 */
  reseed(seed: number): void {
    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
  }

  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(minValue, maxValue);
  }

  get vanilla(): UnifiedRandom {
    return this.ur;
  }
}

// ── Crc32（ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32 反编译 1:1）─────────────────────────────
// 标准 IEEE 表（多项式 0xEDB88320）。按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符：
