 "new_string": "  // ③ 重放（fresh RNG(seed)，槽头 reseed 同构）——ur 层 draw 日志（所有 RNG 入口\n  //    每次调用恰耗 1 个流采样；包装 nextDouble/nextInt/nextIntRange 全捕获）\n  const rng = new RNG(cap.gs.seed);\n  const draws: Array<{ n: number; kind: string; v: number }> = [];\n  const urObj = (rng as unknown as { ur: { nextDouble: () => number; nextInt: (n: number) => number; nextIntRange: (a: number, b: number) => number } }).ur;\n  const rawND = urObj.nextDouble.bind(urObj);\n  const rawNI = urObj.nextInt.bind(urObj);\n  const rawNIR = urObj.nextIntRange.bind(urObj);\n  let logging = true;\n  urObj.nextDouble = () => { const v = rawND(); if (logging) draws.push({ n: draws.length, kind: 'f', v: +v.toFixed(4) }); if (draws.length > 20000) logging = false; return v; };\n  urObj.nextInt = (n: number) => { const v = rawNI(n); if (logging) draws.push({ n: draws.length, kind: `i${n}`, v }); if (draws.length > 20000) logging = false; return v; };\n  urObj.nextIntRange = (a: number, b: number) => { const v = rawNIR(a, b); if (logging) draws.push({ n: draws.length, kind: `r${a}-${b}`, v }); if (draws.length > 20000) logging = false; return v; };"
}

function emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number, ignoreVan = -1): boolean {
  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return false;
  const saplingMode = ignoreVan !== -1 && COMMON_SAPLING_VAN.has(ignoreVan);
  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
      const i = st.idx(x, y);
      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
      const t = st.type[i];
      if (ignoreVan === -1) return false;
      if (ignoreVan === 11 || ignoreVan === 71) { if (!IGNORE_ID_VAN.has(t)) return false; continue; }
      if (saplingMode) {
        if (COMMON_SAPLING.has(t)) continue;
        if (EMPTY_DECOR.has(t)) continue;
        return false;
      }
    }
  }
  return true;
}

 "new_string": "  // ③ 重放（fresh RNG(seed)，槽头 reseed 同构）——ur 层 draw 日志（扫描位钩注上下文）\n  const rng = new RNG(cap.gs.seed);\n  const draws: Array<{ n: number; kind: string; v: number; x: number; y: number; ph: number }> = [];\n  const urObj = (rng as unknown as { ur: { nextDouble: () => number; nextInt: (n: number) => number; nextIntRange: (a: number, b: number) => number } }).ur;\n  const rawND = urObj.nextDouble.bind(urObj);\n  const rawNI = urObj.nextInt.bind(urObj);\n  const rawNIR = urObj.nextIntRange.bind(urObj);\n  const g1 = globalThis as { __swScanCap?: (x: number, y: number, ph: number) => void };\n  let scX = -1, scY = -1, scPh = -1;\n  g1.__swScanCap = (x, y, ph) => { scX = x; scY = y; scPh = ph; };\n  const push = (kind: string, v: number) => draws.push({ n: draws.length, kind, v, x: scX, y: scY, ph: scPh });\n  urObj.nextDouble = () => { const v = rawND(); push('f', +v.toFixed(4)); return v; };\n  urObj.nextInt = (n: number) => { const v = rawNI(n); push(`i${n}`, v); return v; };\n  urObj.nextIntRange = (a: number, b: number) => { const v = rawNIR(a, b); push(`r${a}-${b}`, v); return v; };"
}

  //   `WorldGen.SetCrackedBrickSolidity(solid: false)` → 481/482/483 非实心）。
  //   本仓 TILE_DEFS 已把 481-483 预翻为 solid:false（数据不可动），此处接线仅为
  //   时间线存档——DungeonPass 内部消费端清单见 GenSolid.ts 头注（禁改该文件）。
  //   还原点 = SmoothWorld 头 cs:16510 翻 true（本仓数据无法表达该窗口,备案）→
  //   SmoothWorld 尾 cs:16695 翻回 false（HalfBrickPass 接线）。
  flipGenSolid([481, 482, 483], false);
  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶Dungeon cs:14816（17 内部段不中间重播）
  runDungeonPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 32 Dungeon(蓝/粉砖主题+走廊房间+宝箱)
  // 地牢 pass 预计算(legacy 入口)不迁移 dungeonLocation——但入口塔列仍可能与
  // Reset 期锚点偏差数百格,必须在此回写最终位置。优先取入口门厅可站立点:
  // 生成期 Main.dungeonX/Y 的唯一写入端 = SetOldManSpawnAndSpawnOldManIfDefault
  // Dungeon(DungeonUtils.cs:1665,守卫老人出生/游走锚 = 外前厅 0.5 框底部;
  // WorldGen.cs:72510 那处是运行时兜底 setter,非生成期语义)——塔心锚点会把老人漏进塔内
  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonEntX || gs.dungeonLocation;
  world.dungeonY = gs.dungeonEntY || gs.dungeonY || 0;
  // 33 MountainCaveOpenings + 34 Beaches（原紧跟 SurfaceCaves——注册序修正移到 Dungeon 后）
  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶MountainCaveOpenings cs:14932
  runMountainCavesPass(store, rng, gs);
  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶BeachesAndOceanCleanup cs:14947
  runBeachesPass(store, rng, gs);
  // 海盆挖完后刷新 surface[](后续旧 pass 依赖;本函数之后才到那些 pass)
  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
    }

export function runVinesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const g0 = globalThis as { __swVnCap?: (gs: GenState, st: TileStore) => void };
  try { g0.__swVnCap?.(gs, st); } catch { /* probe */ }
  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
  const grassWalls = new Set([68, 65, 66, 63]);
  for (let i = 5; i < w - 5; i++) {
    // ---- ① 普通藤 52 / 花藤 382（草面/树叶下，地表以上）（cs:20346-20387）----
    let pending = 0;
    let type = VINE;
    const surfLimit = gs.worldSurface;
    for (let j = 0; j < surfLimit; j++) {
      if (pending > 0 && !activeAt(st, i, j)) {
        putVine(st, i, j, type);
        pending--;
      } else {
        pending = 0;
      }
      const sj = sheetAt(st, i, j);
      if (activeAt(st, i, j) && !bottomSlope(st, i, j)
        && (sj === 2 || (sj === 192 && rng.int(0, 3) === 0))            // Next(4)
        && growMoreVines(st, i, j)) {
        type = VINE;
        const wallHere = st.inBounds(i, j) ? st.wall[st.idx(i, j)] : 0;
        const wallBelow = st.inBounds(i, j + 1) ? st.wall[st.idx(i, j + 1)] : 0;
        if (grassWalls.has(wallHere) || grassWalls.has(wallBelow)) type = VINE_FLOWER;
        if (rng.int(0, 4) < 3) pending = rng.int(1, 9);                 // Next(5)/Next(1,10)
      }
    }
    // ---- ② 丛林藤 62（丛林草/蜥蜴砖下，全高）（cs:20389-20511）----
    pending = 0;
    for (let k = 5; k < h - 5; k++) {
      if (pending > 0 && !activeAt(st, i, k)) {
        putVine(st, i, k, VINE_JUNGLE);
        pending--;
      } else {
        pending = 0;
      }
      const sk = sheetAt(st, i, k);
      if (activeAt(st, i, k) && (sk === 60 || sk === 226) && !bottomSlope(st, i, k)
        && growMoreVines(st, i, k)) {
        // 蜂巢凹龛（cs:20453-20501）：右侧也是丛林草时 1/40 放 2×2 蜂巢块
        if (i < w - 1 && k < h - 2 && activeAt(st, i + 1, k)
          && sheetAt(st, i + 1, k) === 60 && !bottomSlope(st, i + 1, k)
          && rng.int(0, 39) === 0) {                                    // Next(40)
          let ok = true;
          for (let a = i; a < i + 2 && ok; a++) {
            for (let b = k + 1; b < k + 3 && ok; b++) {
              if (!st.inBounds(a, b)) { ok = false; break; }
              const kb = st.idx(a, b);
              const sb = SHEET[st.type[kb]];
              if (st.flags[kb] && (!TILE_CUT.has(sb) || sb === 444)) ok = false;
              else if (st.liquid[kb] > 0 || WALL_HOUSE.has(st.wall[kb])) ok = false;
            }
          }
          if (ok && countNearBlocksTypes(st, i, k, 20, 444) > 0) ok = false;
          if (ok) {
            for (let a = i; a < i + 2; a++) {
              for (let b = k + 1; b < k + 3; b++) {
                killTile(st, a, b);
                st.setTileSilent(a, b, HIVE, (a - i) * 18, (b - k - 1) * 18);
              }
            }
            continue;
          }
        }
        let go = true;
        if (sk === 226 && tooManyJungleVines(st, i, k)) go = false;
        if (go && rng.int(0, 4) < 3) pending = rng.int(1, 9);
      }
    }
    // ---- ③ 蘑菇藤 528（蘑菇草 70 下，1/5 起藤）（cs:20514-20531）----
    pending = 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
      if (pending > 0 && !activeAt(st, i, y)) {
        putVine(st, i, y, VINE_MUSHROOM);
        pending--;
      } else {
        pending = 0;
      }
      if (activeAt(st, i, y) && sheetAt(st, i, y) === 70

        pending = rng.int(1, 9);

        putVine(st, i, y, VINE_CORRUPT);

## 165 族漂移清剿 — 结论报告

### 核心发现：任务前提修正

**VV 修复后 s111 真值仍为 165>-1=14,459 / -1>165=14,772（总 29,231）**——与修复前的旧报告（29,024）完全相同。"预期降至 ~15k"所依据的模型（多个独立放置 bug 叠加，修一个少一段）**不成立**。真相是：**#103 之后再无 165 族放置 bug；29k 漂移全部是单一 RNG 流雪崩，引燃点在 pass #53（SmoothWorld）之前**。

### 证据链（seed 9293480，4200×1200，与 /tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g dump 同参对拍）

1. **总量/族列/邻域三对齐**：vanilla 22,670 vs JS 23,117（+2%），所有放置族（small/石族/冰族/硬化沙族）计数一致 → 纯位置漂移。
2. **槽位↔dump 对齐边界对拍**（tools/_165bnd.test.ts）：候选 pass 全部测污——#64 GemCaves +1.2k typ、#65 Moss +4.6k、#92/93 +0.8k、QuickCleanup +0.4k，**全部零直接 165 贡献**；165 漂移从 1.8k→29k 诞生在 #103 槽内部（slot55 前后）。
3. **#103 实现干净——外科手术证明**（tools/_165replay.test.ts）：用 dump102 的原版 typ/act/wal/liq（+JS half/slope 近似通道）重放 #103，首分歧格 (22,671) 输入逐位一致但 vanilla 放置、JS 未放。逐格 draw 审计（ur 层 RNG 包装 + __swScanCap 扫描位钩）定位：**(22,664) JS slope=2 使宝石树门失败、少掷 1 次 height（r7-13）**；仅把该单格 slope 改 0，列 20-23 的 **17 个 vanilla 放置事件 17/17 精确复现**。VV 的 placeTightInitial 无残留 bug。
4. **因果链定案**：slot≤13 时输入已有 20.4k typ/act 差（dump052 边界）→ SmoothWorld（slot13，HalfBrickPass 域）的 **SlopeTile/PoundTile 不改 typ/act，是 dump 不可见的分歧通道** → #103 宝石树门（`tile.slope()!=0`/`halfBrick()` 即返 false）与 `!active` 门翻转 → 每 1 处翻转 = +1 掷偏移 → 从 **x≈22（第 3 列）起全图雪崩**，~90% 放置位置随机化（2×~15k≈29k）。

### 修复落地

- **QuickCleanupPass.ts (b) 段**：QQ 备案坐实并修复——vanilla cs:18017-18018 在沙下填土（type=0）后显式 `halfBrick(false)+slope(0)`，JS `setTileSilent`（非 0 内部 id）不清，已补显式清零。回归验证：caves-checkpoint 失败集修复前后逐 hash 相同（altars/statues/dungeon 族失败是他人域预存），gem-passes 5✓、content-passes-batch 6✓。
- **GemPasses.ts**：新增 `__swScanCap` 扫描位探针钩（同 `__swSpCap` 既有模式，未定义时 no-op）。

|---|---|---|
